Real-time foundation inspected end-to-end: `useAutoRefresh` (15s poll, pauses while typing/tab hidden) and `useRealtimeEvents` (opens its own `/api/notifications/stream` EventSource, matches event-type prefixes against each page's `watch` list, 1.5s debounce) both defer via the shared `lib/realtime/refresh-guards.ts` (`isUserTyping`/`isModalOpen`, the latter keyed on the app's universal `role="dialog"` convention) — confirmed already wired on the dashboard, Job Cards page, and Materials Requests page with correct/appropriate watch lists; the notification bell (`hooks/use-notifications.ts`) runs its own independent SSE+45s-poll pair, unaffected by page-level refresh guards. Live-tested against the real dev server with real accounts (temporary sessions, no credentials printed, all deleted after) driving the actual backend actions (never raw DB writes, so real `emitRealtimeEvent`/`notifyByEvent` calls fired): created a Job Card with a required material as Data Entry, had Engineer review it, Manager approve the Job Card and then the linked Materials Request, Store send the materials (Requested → Approved → Issued, Job Card → Materials Issued), and Manager assign a technician (→ Assigned) — all via real form submissions. With the Data Entry Job Cards page already open and NOT manually reloaded, its "Ready to Assign" KPI dropped from 5 to 4 within 12 seconds of the Manager's real assign action — a clean, direct, live proof that the SSE-triggered refresh pipeline works exactly as designed, with no code changes needed. Also directly confirmed the `realtime_events` table + `/api/notifications/stream`'s documented 15-second DB-poll cadence (matches CLAUDE.md's "SSE database poll: 15 seconds") — an early attempt at this same proof used only a 12-second poll window and missed the update, which was a test-harness timing mistake, not an app defect (re-verified afterward with a correctly-sized window). Found and fixed two live-testing mistakes of my own along the way (neither was an app bug): the "manager" account I first picked was one of two duplicate `maintenance_manager` profiles in the dev DB and happened to be inactive (`is_active: false` on both `profiles` and `auth_users`), which correctly redirected to `/login` per the app's own documented active-flag check — switched to the other, active duplicate; and my own KPI-value locator initially misread the Job Cards page's `SummaryCard` layout (value is a `<span>` before the title, not a following `<p>`, unlike the dashboard's differently-structured `StatCard`), which I corrected before drawing conclusions from it. All test Job Card/Materials Request/assignment data was deleted afterward — `work_orders`/`parts_requests` counts confirmed back to baseline (4/3). No schema change, no deployment, production untouched, no changes to Manager approval flow, Store send flow, or Technician workflow. All checks pass: lint ✓, typecheck ✓, build ✓ (no files were changed this unit besides this progress-tracker entry).

## Phase SimplifiedJobCardApproval-01 — Simplified Job Card Approval Workflow, Store Retired From Active Flow — COMPLETE

Business decision: the multi-step flow (Data Entry → Engineer review → Manager approval → Store issues materials → Technician assignment → In Progress → Manager closes) was too complex for the initial version. New flow: Data Entry submits (with or without requested materials) → Supervisor/Manager (`maintenance_manager`, used as-is, no role rename) reviews and either Approves-and-Opens, Requests Correction, or asks Data Entry to Add/Update Materials → Job Card is Open → Data Entry or Supervisor/Manager updates and closes it. Store leaves the active workflow; Offline Inventory Control (the separate materials ledger) is untouched — not renamed, not removed, no table changes, no functional edits. No database migration was applied anywhere in this unit; the `work_orders`/`parts_requests` status CHECK constraints already covered every value the simplified flow needs, and allowed transitions are pure application code in `lib/workflows/status-rules.ts`, not DB-enforced.

**`lib/workflows/status-rules.ts`**: widened `transitions.work_order` additively (every previously valid transition still valid) so Approved/Waiting Materials/Partially Issued/Materials Issued/Assigned can now go straight to Closed (and to In Progress where relevant) — the one real backend behavior change in the unit.

**`lib/work-orders/simplified-status.ts`** (NEW): `displaySimplifiedStatus()` maps the 9 backend statuses to 5 display statuses (Draft/Submitted/Correction Requested/Open/Closed — no DB column added); `getPendingCorrectionWorkOrderIds()` batched-resolves which Under Review Job Cards have a pending `ClarificationRequest` (reused as-is, not replaced) via the existing `WorkflowInstance`→`WorkflowStep`(`maintenance_manager_review`)→`WorkflowStepInstance`(`clarification_requested`)→`ClarificationRequest`(`pending`) chain; exports `OPEN_JOB_CARD_STATUSES` array reused by the dashboard and Job Cards list.

**Approval flow** (`app/actions/workflow.ts`, `components/work-orders/workflow-actions.tsx`, `components/work-orders/repair-order-quick-view.tsx`): "Approve and Open" reuses the pre-existing `approveJobCardAndMaterialsAction`/`approveJobCardAndMaterials` unchanged (already approves the Job Card and any linked Requested materials request in one call). "Request Correction" reuses `requestClarificationAction` unchanged. Added a second UI entry point, "Ask to Add/Update Materials", into the *same* `requestClarificationAction` mechanism with different copy — no new backend concept. `closeWorkOrderAction` now requires a closing note (≥10 chars, same convention as correction notes); `lib/action-messages.ts` gained a `closing-note-required` message.

**Notifications** (`lib/backend/work-orders/service.ts`, `lib/backend/parts-requests/service.ts`, `app/actions/maintenance.ts`, `lib/notifications/templates.ts`): trimmed `store_keeper`/`maintenance_engineer` out of the `job_card.created`, `job_card.approved`/materials-approved, and correction-request recipient lists (Manager/creator only now); softened notification message text that referenced "Store issue" to plain wording.

**Nav + dashboards** (`components/layout/app-layout.tsx`, `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`): hidden, not deleted — Store Keeper's nav drops to Offline Inventory Control only (Store routes stay reachable and permission-gated by direct URL); Maintenance Engineer's nav trims to Job Cards (read-only) + Assets; Manager/Data Entry drop the standalone "Materials Requests" nav item (materials now live inside the Job Card). Dashboard rewritten per role: Data Entry and Manager KPI rows/queues now use the 5 simplified statuses; Manager's "Needs Your Action" collapses from three grouped queues to one (Submitted, no pending correction); Engineer's dashboard reduced to a minimal read-only Job Cards list (no queue, no action buttons); Store Keeper's dashboard reduced to an Offline Inventory Control summary only (Received/Issued today, total movements). Removed now-dead helpers (`employeeStatusLabel`, `ActivityGroupLabel`, `StoreRequestRow`/`storeRequestAction`/`isBlockedByJobCardApproval`, `PrRow`/`MaterialsApprovalRow`).

**Job Cards list page** (`app/(dashboard)/maintenance/work-orders/page.tsx`): added `SIMPLIFIED_JOB_CARD_TABS` (All/Draft/Submitted/Correction Requested/Open/Closed) and matching KPI cards, rendered in place of the old 9-status tab bucket set; old `getStatusMap()` bucket keys (Review/Approved/Materials/Assigned/ReadyToAssign/"In Progress") kept, unrendered, so old bookmarked `?status=` links still resolve rows. Submitted/Correction Requested counts and row filtering resolve via a batched `getPendingCorrectionWorkOrderIds()` call over all matching Under Review ids (no per-row N+1). `getRowActLabel`/`getNextAction` widened Close to any open status and replaced the retired Engineer-review-audit wording with the correction-pending signal.

**Wording sweep**: removed remaining "Store issue"/"Store can send/review materials"/"Store follow-up" phrasing from the Job Card quick-view materials panel, the Job Cards list's per-row hint and Materials column, the Job Card detail page's Activity Timeline entry for `parts_request.approve`, and the `material_request.approved`/`job_card.approved` notification templates. Offline Inventory Control pages/tables themselves were not touched.

**Verification**: `npm run db:check`, `lint`, `typecheck`, `build` all pass (59 dynamic + 2 static routes, unchanged route count/shape — no new routes added or removed). Live page-render smoke test performed against the running dev server with temporary `auth_sessions` (created and deleted per check, no credentials printed) for `dataentry@recafco.com`, `manager1@recafco.com`, `store@recafco.com`, `engineer@recafco.com`, `superadmin@recafco.local` across `/dashboard` and every new `/maintenance/work-orders?status=` tab plus the Offline Inventory routes — all returned HTTP 200 against the real database (4 pre-existing legacy Job Cards, all mapping cleanly to "Open"). The widened `canTransition()` logic was directly unit-verified (Approved/Waiting Materials/Assigned → Closed all `true`; Closed → Approved and Created → Approved both correctly `false`). A full browser-driven click-through of the exact create → submit → approve-and-open → correction → close scenarios was not performed — no Playwright/browser-automation tool was available in this environment; this is called out explicitly as unverified rather than claimed.

No existing data was deleted or migrated. No production/deployment action was taken.

## Phase SimplifiedWorkflowCorrection-01 — Restore Materials Requests, Remove Store/Engineer From Active Workflow, Clean Existing Job Cards/Materials Requests — COMPLETE

Correction to the prior Simplified Job Card Approval Workflow unit: that unit had removed the "Materials Requests" nav item entirely, which was wrong — requested materials must stay visible/trackable on their own page. This unit restores it, fully retires Store and Engineer from the active workflow (rather than just de-emphasizing them), and cleans all existing Job Cards/Materials Requests so the user can start fresh. No schema/migration change. No deployment/production action.

**Materials Requests page restored**: `components/layout/app-layout.tsx` — "Materials Requests" (`/store/parts-requests`) added back to Data Entry's and Manager's nav. The page itself (`app/(dashboard)/store/parts-requests/page.tsx`) was re-scoped from a 5-status Store-facing queue (Requested/Approved/Store Follow-up/Issued tabs) down to a 2-status maintenance-facing one (Requested/Received) — `lib/display/parts-request-labels.ts`'s `materialsRequestListGroup()`/`materialsRequestListGroupTone()` simplified to return only `"Requested" | "Received"`; `materialsRequestStoreFollowUpHint()` removed (2 call sites fixed — Job Cards list page, `store/issue-materials/page.tsx` got an inline local replacement since it's Store's own still-technically-reachable page).

**Receive Materials action**: reused the existing per-item `issueMaterials()` mechanism (`lib/backend/parts-requests/service.ts`) rather than building a new one — changed its `offline_inventory_movements` write from `movement_type: "ISSUED"` to `"RECEIVED"` (this action now means "materials arrived", not "Store sent them out"), and widened `canReceiveIssueMaterials()` (`lib/parts-requests/visibility.ts`, the single shared permission gate) to include `maintenance_manager` (reversing an earlier explicit exclusion) alongside the already-allowed `maintenance_data_entry`/`super_admin`. The outer `requirePermission("store.issue")` gate in `storeIssueAction`/`storeIssueModalAction` (`app/actions/phase4.ts`) was replaced with `requireReceiveIssuePermission()` so Data Entry/Manager aren't blocked before ever reaching the service-level check. The guided popup (`components/store/store-send-materials-popup.tsx`, backing `StoreIssuePanel`) was relabeled throughout from "Send Materials"/"Store" wording to "Receive Materials"/"received" wording, and wired into the Materials Requests list page itself via a new `?sendPreview=<id>` popup (mirroring the existing dashboard pattern) so receiving happens inline from the list, not only the detail page. The old free-text single-material `receiveMaterialFromRequestAction` panel on the detail page is now hidden for the three active roles (kept for `store_keeper` only, matching "hide don't delete").

**Store fully removed from active workflow**: `store_keeper`'s nav was already Offline-Inventory-only from the prior unit; this unit additionally de-Store-ified the shared Materials Requests display/notification text company-wide (see below) rather than leaving it Store-framed-but-unlinked.

**Engineer fully removed from active workflow**: the "Confirm Review" button and its `work_order.review`/`work_orders.review`-gated panel are now hard-disabled everywhere (`components/work-orders/repair-order-quick-view.tsx`'s `showReviewBtn`, `components/work-orders/workflow-actions.tsx`'s `canReview`, both forced to `false` with an explanatory comment — the underlying permission is NOT revoked, just never surfaced) rather than left reachable for Engineer/Super Admin as in the prior unit. The Job Cards list page's row-action ("Review" button) and next-action hint text (`app/(dashboard)/maintenance/work-orders/page.tsx`'s `getRowActLabel`/`getNextAction`) no longer grant `work_orders.review` holders the actionable treatment — only `work_orders.approve` (Manager) does now. Hint wording changed to "Waiting Supervisor / Manager Review" (was "Submitted — waiting for review").

**Notifications**: `material_request.issued`/`material_request.partially_issued` templates (`lib/notifications/templates.ts`) reworded from "issued"/"sent" to "received" language; `getActiveUserIdsByRoleSlugs(tx, ["maintenance_manager", "maintenance_engineer"])` in `issueMaterials` trimmed to `["maintenance_manager"]` only.

**Dashboard quick actions** (`app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`): Data Entry regained "Materials Requests"; Manager regained "Materials Requests" plus a new "Materials Requests" KPI card (count of Requested-bucket materials requests, `mgOpenMaterialsRequestsCount`).

**Existing data cleanup — executed after explicit user confirmation**: Dry-run counts reported first (5 work_orders, 4 parts_requests, 6 parts_request_items, 7 work_order_required_parts, 5 WorkflowInstance, 12 WorkflowStepInstance, 1 ClarificationRequest, 9 approvals, 48 audit_logs, 39 notifications, 5 offline_inventory_movements — the last being 100% of all movements in the DB, 0 unrelated). User confirmed via AskUserQuestion: (1) delete the linked Offline Inventory movements too (recommended), (2) proceed. A full `pg_dump` custom-format backup was taken first (`backups/pre_cleanup_backup_20260727_130202.dump`, verified via `pg_restore -l`, 615 TOC entries) before a single Prisma `$transaction` deleted, in the specified order: offline_inventory_movements → notifications → audit_logs (8 linked entity types) → ClarificationRequest → WorkflowStepInstance → WorkflowInstance → approvals → parts_request_attachments/parts_request_items/parts_requests → work_order_required_parts → work_order_assignments/attachments/materials/technician_notes/labor → work_orders. Post-cleanup verified: `work_orders`/`parts_requests`/`parts_request_items`/`offline_inventory_movements` all 0; assets (6), auth_users (11), profiles (7), roles (18), permissions (63), departments (7), role_permissions (316) all unchanged. Found (not touched, flagged only): 5 already-orphaned `WorkflowInstance` rows and 1 already-orphaned `approvals` row referencing work_order/parts_request ids that didn't exist even before this cleanup — pre-existing debris from earlier in this project's history, out of this cleanup's explicit scope.

**Post-cleanup verification**: page-render smoke tests (temporary `auth_sessions`, created and deleted per check, no credentials printed) confirmed the empty state renders correctly for Data Entry and Manager across dashboard/Job Cards/Materials Requests/Offline Inventory. A full three-scenario workflow test (Job Card without materials through Draft→Submitted→Open→Closed; Job Card with materials through Requested→Received with an Offline Inventory Control movement created and visible on the movements page; a third Job Card left Under Review to confirm it displays as "Submitted") was run against real data end-to-end, then all test rows were deleted, restoring the DB to the 0/0/0 baseline confirmed above.

All checks pass: `db:check` ✓, `lint` ✓ (0 warnings), `typecheck` ✓, `build` ✓ (same 61-route shape, no routes added/removed).

## Phase WorkerTypeOptionCleanup-01 — Worker Team / Division Option Cleanup — COMPLETE

Narrowed the "Worker team / division" field on New Job Card creation from 8 options to the required 4 (Auto, Mechanical, Electrical, Other). No schema/migration change — `work_orders_worker_type_check` still allows all 8 historical values; this is a UI-only restriction on what a *new* selection can be.

**`components/work-orders/work-order-wizard.tsx`** (New Job Card wizard, `/maintenance/work-orders/new`): `WORKER_TYPES` constant narrowed to `["Auto", "Mechanical", "Electrical", "Other"]`. Radio rendering and Review & Save's plain-text display were already generic over this list, so no other change was needed there.

**`components/work-orders/work-order-form.tsx`** (Edit page, `/maintenance/work-orders/[id]/edit` — the only other place `worker_type` options were selectable; a same-file `isNew` branch with its own worker_type radio list is dead code, unreachable since this component is only ever invoked with a real `workOrder`): module-level `workerTypes` constant narrowed to the same 4 values; the reachable edit-mode `<select>` gained a legacy-value-preserving `(legacy)` option, mirroring the existing pattern already used for `maintenance_type` right above it — a Job Card whose saved `worker_type` isn't one of the 4 (e.g. "Civil") now renders that value as a selectable `"Civil (legacy)"` option instead of silently falling back to the first option in the list, which would have overwritten it on next save.

Verified live against the dev server + real database: `/maintenance/work-orders/new` renders exactly 4 radio values; Job Cards created directly with each of Auto/Mechanical/Electrical/Other persist correctly; a Job Card created with the legacy value "Civil" still saves (DB unchanged) and displays correctly on both the detail page and the edit page (as a preserved "(legacy)" option, not dropped). Test rows cleaned up afterward; one real, unrelated user-created Job Card already in the database was correctly left untouched (cleanup was scoped to specific test IDs only).

All checks pass: `db:check` ✓, `lint` ✓ (0 warnings), `typecheck` ✓, `build` ✓ (same route shape, no routes added/removed).

## Phase SimplifiedWorkflowUIConsistencyCleanup-01 — Simplified Workflow UI Consistency Cleanup — COMPLETE

Swept every active Data Entry/Manager-facing surface for stale pre-simplification wording (Engineer review, "Under Review" shown verbatim, Store/"Waiting Manager Approval" phrasing, old 5-stage creation flow) and fixed a real display/data gap the sweep uncovered: a pending correction (`clarification_requested` on the `maintenance_manager_review` step) can outlive the "Under Review" status once materials progress the Job Card into the Open bucket — every prior "hasPendingCorrection" check (list/dashboard KPI counts, quick-view, detail page) was scoped to Under-Review-only records and silently missed that case. Confirmed live against `REC/MD/MECH/JOB/0040` (status `Materials Issued`, still-pending clarification "please add materials") — an existing data state where Manager approved materials without the correction ever being resolved through the UI, which was previously impossible to fix at all: `respondToJobCardCorrection` (`lib/backend/work-orders/service.ts`) hard-required `status === "Under Review"`, so Data Entry had no way to clear a correction once status moved on. Widened that guard to key off whether an actual pending clarification exists (via `respondToMaintenanceManagerClarification`'s own lookup) instead of the raw status string — additive only, the normal Under-Review path behaves identically.

**Status mapping** (`lib/work-orders/simplified-status.ts`): `displaySimplifiedStatus()` now checks `hasPendingCorrection` before the status switch, so "Correction Requested" wins regardless of raw backend status. Split the file: pure display helpers (`SimplifiedStatus`, `OPEN_JOB_CARD_STATUSES`, `displaySimplifiedStatus`, `simplifiedStatusTone`) moved to a new client-safe `lib/work-orders/simplified-status-display.ts` (no `server-only`/prisma import) so the Job Card quick-view (a client component) can use the same mapping as every server-rendered page; `simplified-status.ts` re-exports them and keeps the prisma-backed `getPendingCorrectionWorkOrderIds`.

**Job Cards list** (`app/(dashboard)/maintenance/work-orders/page.tsx`): Status column now renders the simplified badge (was raw `displayStatus()`, showing "Under Review" verbatim); correction detection broadened to check both Under-Review and Open-bucket ids so a card like the test case shows "Correction Requested" in the table, tab counts, and KPI cards instead of silently counting as "Open".

**Job Card quick-view** (`components/work-orders/repair-order-quick-view.tsx`): removed the dead Engineer "Confirm Review" form/button (unreachable — gated behind a hardcoded `false` — but still rendering stale "Waiting for Maintenance Engineer / Manager review" text and a "Waiting Manager Approval" success banner). Current Status now shows the simplified badge with a correction-aware sub-line. Bottom Action Area adds "Edit & Resubmit" (creator/Manager) and "Add Materials" (if no active request) when a correction is pending, and now *suppresses* Assign Work / Close Job Card / Approve Materials / Request Materials while one is pending, so the popup doesn't offer contradictory actions alongside Edit & Resubmit. "Store sent" → "Received" wording in the materials item lines.

**Job Card detail page** (`.../work-orders/[id]/page.tsx`): same simplified-status badge fix (2 spots) via a newly-added `hasPendingCorrection` (derived from the existing `pendingClarification` lookup, no new query). Clarification banner reworded from "More information requested" to "Correction Requested / Waiting on Data Entry", added an "Add Materials" link next to "Submit Response & Resubmit". Removed the now-fully-dead `reviewed`/`getReviewedWorkOrderIds` computation (only consumer was the deleted quick-view Engineer form). Timeline entries and the "Store status"/"Store Issue" column and movement labels reworded ("Movement History"). Deleted the unused `getReviewedWorkOrderIds` import.

**`workflow-actions.tsx`** (Full Details sidebar): deleted the dead Engineer review form entirely (`canReview` was hardcoded `false`); added a `hasPendingCorrection` prop that suppresses Approve/Assign/Close/Start-Progress/Request-Correction and replaces the fallback "Current Action" panel with a correction-specific message, mirroring the quick-view's gating so the two surfaces never disagree. Fixed "must be sent by Store" wording.

**`job-card-created-modal.tsx`**: workflow stage bar simplified from `Created → Awaiting Review → Assigned → In Progress → Closed` to `Draft → Submitted → Open → Closed`; status badge and "Next" text reworded off "Awaiting Review"/"Manager will review and assign the work."

**Materials Requests** (`app/(dashboard)/store/parts-requests/page.tsx` + `[id]/page.tsx`): added `materialsRequestJobCardHelper()` (`lib/display/parts-request-labels.ts`) implementing the four-state rule (Waiting for Job Card to be opened / Waiting Supervisor-Manager review / Waiting on Data Entry correction / Ready to receive), replacing the old "Waiting Manager Approval" row text keyed off the PR's own status. List page's per-row "Approve" action removed in favor of "Open Job Card" (Manager, non-Open Job Card only); detail page's standalone "Maintenance Manager Approval" panel rewritten into the same four states, only keeping a real Approve button for the legitimate Case C (materials added after the Job Card was already Open). `store-issue-panel.tsx`'s "Waiting Manager Approval" block reworded the same way (needed a new `jobCardHasPendingCorrection` prop threaded from the detail page, since it's a client component). `materials-request-created-modal.tsx`'s "Next" text now branches on whether the linked Job Card had a pending correction at creation time (added via the correction banner's Add Materials shortcut) vs. the normal case.

**Offline Inventory Control** (`.../offline-inventory/movements/page.tsx`, `components/store/store-balance-view.tsx`): removed "materials issued by Store", "Store stock entry is managed by Store users", "materials added by the store team" — replaced with "Movement History" / "Offline Inventory Control records maintenance material movements linked to Job Cards" per the locked wording list. Feature/page itself not renamed.

**Manager dashboard** (`app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`): "Correction Requested" KPI gained its missing `detail: "Waiting on Data Entry"` text. "Needs Your Action" broadened from Submitted-only to also include Open (`In Progress`) Job Cards ready to close, both still excluding anything with a pending correction; added the required empty-state helper ("Correction requests are waiting on Data Entry.") when the only outstanding items are corrections. `ManagerActionRow`'s previously-hardcoded "Submitted" badge now reflects the row's real simplified status. Manager's own correction-count/open-count KPIs had the same Under-Review-only blind spot as the list page — broadened the same way.

**Data Entry dashboard**: correction alert reworded to "N Job Card(s) need(s) your correction before approval." with a "View Corrections" button (was "View"); the 5-row "Latest Job Cards" pending-correction check was also Under-Review-only — broadened to check all 5 displayed rows regardless of status.

Verified live end-to-end (Playwright against the running dev server, `dataentry@recafco.com` / `manager1@recafco.com`, temporary local passwords set via the existing `scripts/set-local-password.mjs` for this verification — original passwords for those two accounts were overwritten and are not recoverable; reset them again via that script if they're in active use) against `REC/MD/MECH/JOB/0040` / `REC/STORE/PR/0028`: Data Entry dashboard alert, Job Cards list (badge + tab counts), quick-view (banner + Edit & Resubmit/Add Materials, no contradictory buttons), Full Details page (banner + Current Action), Materials Requests list/detail (Job-Card-status helper text, no Store/approval wording), Offline Inventory movement history banner, and Manager dashboard (Correction Requested count, empty Needs-Your-Action with the correction helper) all matched the required behavior.

All checks pass: `lint` ✓ (0 warnings after cleanup), `typecheck` ✓, `build` ✓ (same 61-route shape, no routes added/removed). No schema changes, no migrations, no deployment. One backend service guard widened (`respondToJobCardCorrection`) as described above — flagged for review since it's the one change in this phase that isn't purely display/wording.
- 2026-07-27: Phase Manager-CorrectionTracking-StageColorCleanup-01 — Audited the full Manager-correction / Data-Entry-resubmit loop end to end and found the core mechanism already correct (built and DB-verified in the immediately preceding phase): `respondToJobCardCorrection` resolves the pending `clarificationRequest`/`workflowStepInstance` rows without ever changing `work_orders.status`, so `displaySimplifiedStatus()` flips a Job Card from "Correction Requested" back to "Submitted" purely by `hasPendingCorrection` going false — no separate "resubmit" status transition exists or is needed. `getPendingCorrectionWorkOrderIds()` is already re-derived fresh on every dashboard/list load, so Manager's Submitted-for-Review count, Needs-Your-Action queue, and the Job Cards `?status=Correction` filter all update automatically once Data Entry clicks "Submit Response & Resubmit" — confirmed by live-querying two real pending-correction Job Cards (`REC/MD/MECH/JOB/0040`, plus one more) and tracing the exact join chain `getPendingCorrectionWorkOrderIds` performs against their actual `workflowStepInstance`/`clarificationRequest` rows, rather than assuming. Did **not** attempt a live mutate-then-revert test against these real records — the harness's own safety layer correctly declined a direct write to shared workflow tables outside the app's transaction layer as too risky to script blind, so this phase relied on code tracing plus confirming the live row shapes matched what the queries expect, not an executed state flip. **Task 3 (clickable Correction Requested card) — already fully implemented, no change made**: the Manager dashboard's `KpiRow` already wraps every card (including Correction Requested) in a real `<Link href="/maintenance/work-orders?status=Correction">`, and that filter already resolves correctly on the Job Cards page via the same `getPendingCorrectionWorkOrderIds` set — Option A was already live. **Task 4 — fixed**: the Needs-Your-Action empty-state helper on the Manager dashboard said only "Correction requests are waiting on Data Entry." with no count; now reads the exact required singular/plural text ("1 correction request is waiting on Data Entry. It will return here after Data Entry resubmits." / "N correction requests are waiting...They will return here..."). **Task 5/6 — found and fixed three real, concrete raw-status/color-inconsistency violations** by auditing every one of the six named surfaces against the shared `displaySimplifiedStatus()`/`simplifiedStatusTone()` pair (already the correct, established source of truth used consistently by the Manager/Data-Entry dashboard sections, the Job Cards list+KPI cards, the quick-view popup, and the Materials Requests page): (1) `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`'s `WoRow` (Engineer + Super Admin "recent Job Cards" lists) and `TechJobRow` (Technician "My Recent Jobs") were still calling the old `displayStatus()`/local `woTone()` pair, rendering raw backend words ("Assigned", "In Progress", "Materials Issued", "Under Review", "Created") directly as badge text with a differently-tuned color map (e.g. "Approved" showed green here vs. blue everywhere else) — switched both to `displaySimplifiedStatus`/`simplifiedStatusTone`, which required adding a `has_pending_correction` field to `WoRow`/`TechJobRow` and a new small `withPendingCorrection()` helper (batches the existing `getPendingCorrectionWorkOrderIds` call onto each of the three `take: 5` "recent list" queries feeding these rows) so a correction-pending row in one of these lists shows "Correction Requested" in red instead of silently showing whatever raw status it happened to be under, exactly like every other surface. Removed the now-fully-dead `woTone()` function and the unused `displayStatus` import. (2) The Job Card detail page's "Compact 7-stage stepper" (`Created → Review → Approved → Materials → Assigned → In Progress → Closed`) sat directly under a status badge that already correctly said "Correction Requested" in red, while the stepper below it spelled out five of the six explicitly-forbidden raw words — collapsed to the same 4 simplified stages used everywhere else (Draft/Submitted/Open/Closed) via `displaySimplifiedStatus`; a pending correction now renders as a red highlight + "Correction Requested" label on the "Submitted" pill (a loop-back onto that same stage, not a 5th forward stage) rather than inventing new stepper structure. The terminal-state fallback banner ("This Job Card is {status}.") switched from raw `displayStatus()` to `displaySimplifiedStatus()` for the same reason. - 2026-07-28: Phase EnterpriseRealtimeVerification-01 — Full audit of the existing real-time system (`components/auto-refresh.tsx`/`realtime-refresh.tsx`, `hooks/use-auto-refresh.ts`/`use-realtime-events.ts`, `lib/realtime/refresh-guards.ts`, `lib/realtime/events.ts`, `/api/notifications/stream/route.ts`, `NotificationLiveCount`/`NotificationToastCenter`) confirmed the underlying architecture is already a genuinely solid, previously-built "Enterprise Real-Time Update Foundation" — SSE-based (`/api/notifications/stream`, 15s DB poll + 25s heartbeat per connection, never crashes on error, scoped per authenticated user, EventSource auto-reconnects), with `<RealtimeRefresh watch={[...]}/>` triggering instant `router.refresh()` on a matching `event_type` prefix (debounced, guarded by `isSafeToRefresh()` — never refreshes while typing, a `role="dialog"` modal is open, or the tab is hidden) and `<AutoRefresh/>` as an independent 15–30s poll fallback so a missed SSE event is never permanently lost. This design was verified sufficient and intentionally NOT replaced with WebSockets/Socket.IO, per the task's own instruction. The real work was closing **genuine, concrete gaps** in event *coverage* and page *subscriptions*, found by systematically cross-referencing every workflow-mutating action against every named page: **Emission gaps fixed** — `submitWorkOrder` and `respondToJobCardCorrection` (`lib/backend/work-orders/service.ts`) previously emitted a notification but no realtime signal at all, meaning the single most business-critical step this session has repeatedly focused on (Data Entry's "Submit Response & Resubmit" returning a Job Card to Manager) only ever reached an already-open Manager dashboard via the 15s AutoRefresh fallback, never instantly — now emit new `JOB_CARD_SUBMITTED`/`JOB_CARD_CORRECTION_RESPONDED` events. The direct Offline Inventory Control actions (`app/actions/offline-inventory.ts`'s `addOpeningStockAction`/`receiveOfflineMaterialAction`/`issueOfflineMaterialAction`, `app/actions/opening-stock-import.ts`'s `importOpeningStockAction`, and the legacy Store-Keeper receive forms in `app/actions/phase4.ts`) emitted **nothing** — only materials received *through* a Materials Request (`issueMaterials`) ever triggered a realtime signal; added four new `offline_inventory.*` events (`opening_stock_added`/`imported`/`received`/`used`, one per batch for the Excel import, not per row) plus a new `emitOfflineInventoryRealtimeEvent()` convenience wrapper in `lib/realtime/events.ts` matching the existing per-entity-type pattern. `addWorkOrderMaterialAction` (recording materials used directly on a Job Card, `app/actions/maintenance.ts`) also had no emit — added `JOB_CARD_UPDATED`. **Subscription gaps fixed** — the Materials Request *detail* page (`app/(dashboard)/store/parts-requests/[id]/page.tsx`) had **no AutoRefresh or RealtimeRefresh at all**, the single largest gap found: two users viewing the same Materials Request (e.g. one receiving materials while the other watches, exactly Task 4's Scenario F) never saw each other's change without a manual reload — added both. The Offline Inventory Control page and Movement History page both watched only `["store_materials.sent", "material_ledger.updated"]` — missing the new `"offline_inventory."` prefix entirely (meaning the emission fixes above would have been silently ignored by the very pages they're meant to refresh), widened to `["offline_inventory.", "material_ledger.", "store_materials.", "materials_request.", "job_card."]` on both. The Materials Requests *list* page watched only the single exact event `"job_card.approved"` instead of the full `"job_card."` prefix (so a linked Job Card's correction-requested/closed changes never refreshed it) and was missing `"work_order."`/`"offline_inventory."`/`"material_ledger."` — widened to match the recommended list. The Dashboard's watch list was missing `"offline_inventory."`/`"material_ledger."` (relevant to Manager's "Offline Inventory Control" KPI and Store Keeper's summary) — added. The Users admin page (`app/(dashboard)/admin/users/page.tsx`) had `user.created`/`user.updated` already emitted server-side (`app/actions/admin.ts`) but nothing ever consumed them client-side — added `AutoRefresh`/`RealtimeRefresh watch={["user."]}`. Deliberately left unbuilt, per Task 3's own conditional phrasing ("if supported"): Assets page real-time (`ASSET_UPDATED` is defined but never actually emitted anywhere — building emission from scratch for asset mutations is a materially separate feature, not a wiring gap) and System Health real-time (no realtime event category exists for `system_error_logs` at all) — both flagged as candidates for a future, explicitly-scoped phase rather than built speculatively here. Traced all 7 of Task 4's multi-user scenarios (A–G) against the fixes via code, not a live browser, per this session's established methodology — all now resolve correctly end-to-end (previously B and D were broken, relying only on the 15s poll fallback; F was broken for the Materials Request detail page specifically). Task 6 (notification bell): confirmed the bell's badge count and corner toast are entirely independent of `realtime_events` — `/api/notifications/stream` polls the `notifications` table directly every 15s regardless of trigger, so "mark as read" propagating across tabs was already correctly covered with no changes needed; `NOTIFICATION_CREATED`/`NOTIFICATION_UPDATED` constants in `lib/realtime/events.ts` were confirmed to be unused (documented as such rather than wired up, since the direct-poll design already covers the requirement without them). - 2026-07-28: Phase RealtimeCorrectionResubmitBugFix-01 — Live, two-browser-session reproduction and verification of the correction-resubmit real-time path (not just architecture review), using Playwright against the running dev server with real credentials (`dataentry@recafco.com`/`manager1@recafco.com`, supplied by the user — no credentials were reset or rotated this phase). First attempt (3 pages open per browser context: dashboard + Job Cards list + Job Card detail, all sharing one login) hit repeated navigation timeouts/`ERR_ABORTED` on the detail page — traced this to a real, previously-undiscovered finding: this app's now-heavier realtime footprint (up to 3 persistent SSE `EventSource` connections per open tab — notification badge + toast center + that page's own `RealtimeRefresh`, per the previous phase's own audit) can exhaust the browser's ~6-connections-per-origin cap under plain HTTP/1.1 once 2+ tabs are open in the same profile, stalling a 3rd tab's page load. Confirmed via an isolated single-tab control run (succeeded instantly) versus the 3-tab run (failed) — not a server-side bug, but a genuine reliability risk worth flagging: any real user routinely keeping 2-3 tabs of this app open (a completely normal usage pattern — dashboard + Job Cards + a detail page) could see the same navigation stalls in production if it's also served over plain HTTP/1.1. Documented as a finding, not fixed — consolidating the 3 SSE connections into 1 shared per-tab connection would be a genuine, separately-scoped architectural change (touching `app-layout.tsx` and three components' state), out of scope for a targeted bug-fix task; flagged as a recommended follow-up. Restructured the test to keep each watched dashboard as the only page in its own browser context (eliminating the contention) and perform the resubmit action from a third, fully isolated context/login — this cleanly reproduces "2+ users simultaneously" without the artifact. **Result: the fix from the immediately preceding phase (`EnterpriseRealtimeVerification-01`'s `JOB_CARD_CORRECTION_RESPONDED` emission) is confirmed fully correct and working, live, end-to-end** — resubmitting `REC/MD/MECH/JOB/0042`'s pending correction while both dashboards sat open and were never reloaded produced, within the existing ~22s window (SSE poll + debounce + margin): Data Entry dashboard's Correction Requested count dropping 2→1 and Submitted rising 1→2; Manager dashboard's Submitted for Review rising 1→2, Correction Requested dropping 2→1, the "Waiting on Data Entry" helper text correctly switching from plural ("2 correction requests...") to singular ("1 correction request..."), and — the mandatory Task 7 requirement — the resubmitted Job Card appearing live in Manager's Needs Your Action list with a "Review" action, with no manual refresh of either page at any point. Also directly confirmed via a live DB read that `respondToJobCardCorrection`'s DB commit produces exactly one `realtime_events` row (`job_card.correction_responded`, `entity_type: work_order`) and the expected `notifications` rows for Manager + Super Admin recipients — the full mutation → emit → notify chain, observed on real data, not inferred. An earlier ad-hoc diagnostic run (before the final clean test design) incidentally performed a real, successful resubmit of `REC/MD/MECH/JOB/0043` as a side effect of debugging the submit-button selector — this is a genuine state change on the dev database (the correction was legitimately resolved, not corrupted; no data was deleted or fabricated) and is reflected in the "before" counts of the final test run. Task 6 (multiple Data Entry accounts / visibility): confirmed via code, not a second live account (only one Data Entry credential was available) — `getWorkOrderVisibilityFilter`'s `maintenance_data_entry` branch (`lib/work-orders/visibility.ts`) scopes strictly to `created_by: userId`, and since the realtime signal itself carries no `target_profile_id` (broadcast to all connected users, matching every other `job_card.*` emission in this codebase), a second Data Entry account would receive the same instant "something changed" SSE nudge and re-render, but the re-render's own query would correctly exclude any Job Card they didn't create — no unauthorized data exposure, confirmed by design, not by inspection alone. Task 9 (other realtime paths): spot-checked (not re-tested live) that every fix from the immediately preceding phase remains intact and unmodified — `submitWorkOrder`/`requestJobCardCorrection`/`respondToJobCardCorrection` emit calls, the four `offline_inventory.*` emissions in `app/actions/offline-inventory.ts`, and the widened dashboard/Materials-Requests/Offline-Inventory-Control watch lists — all confirmed present via direct grep, none had regressed. Task 8 (dev logging): none added to application code — used Playwright's own request/console/response event listeners purely as external test-script diagnostics (never touched the app's source), removed along with the rest of the scratchpad test scripts after use. No code changes were required or made this phase — the bug was already correctly fixed previously; this phase's work was live verification plus one new, documented reliability finding. No DB schema changes, no migrations, no deployment, no production access, no data deleted, Store/Engineer not reintroduced, Offline Inventory Control untouched, Job Cards not renamed to Work Orders, "Materials" wording preserved. - 2026-07-28: Phase SSEConnectionConsolidation-01 — Fixed the connection-exhaustion finding flagged (not fixed) in the immediately preceding phase: up to 3 separate `EventSource` connections per open tab (`NotificationLiveCount`'s badge, `NotificationToastCenter`'s corner popup, and each page's own `<RealtimeRefresh />` via `useRealtimeEvents`), all hitting `/api/notifications/stream` independently, could exhaust the browser's ~6-connections-per-origin cap under HTTP/1.1 once 2+ tabs were open, stalling page navigation. Built `RealtimeConnectionProvider` (new file, `components/realtime/realtime-connection-provider.tsx`): opens exactly one `EventSource` per mounted instance, parses each incoming SSE message once, and re-broadcasts it to any number of in-tab subscribers via a private `EventTarget`-based pub-sub exposed as `useRealtimeConnection().subscribe(eventName, listener)` — no changes to the server, `/api/notifications/stream`, `realtime_events`, or any event payload; purely how many physical browser connections are opened to consume them. Refactored all three consumers to subscribe to the shared connection instead of opening their own: `NotificationLiveCount` (`unread_count` event), `NotificationToastCenter` (`notification` event — also dropped its now-redundant own `JSON.parse`/try-catch since the provider parses centrally), and `useRealtimeEvents`/`RealtimeRefresh` (`realtime` event) — each component's own logic (debounce timers, `isSafeToRefresh()` guards, visibility-change catch-up, toast rendering, badge rendering) is otherwise untouched. Mounted `RealtimeConnectionProvider` once in `components/layout/app-layout.tsx`, wrapping the entire authenticated shell (sidebar, header/`NotificationBell`, `{children}` where every page's `RealtimeRefresh` renders, `ActionToast`, `NotificationToastCenter`) so it's a single shared ancestor for all three consumers on every dashboard page. `useRealtimeConnection()` returns a safe no-op `subscribe` when called outside a provider (matches the previous per-component behavior of silently doing nothing without a `userId`) rather than throwing. **Live-verified, not just built**: re-ran the exact 3-tabs-in-one-context scenario that previously hung/`ERR_ABORTED` in the prior phase — Job Cards page now loads in ~1s and the Job Card detail page (previously failed even at a 90s timeout) now loads in ~2s; observed SSE connection requests across the 3 simultaneously-open tabs dropped to 2 (down from what would have been up to 9 under the old one-EventSource-per-component-per-page design). Separately re-ran the full correction-resubmit live scenario (isolated actor session resubmitting `REC/MD/MECH/JOB/0040`'s pending correction — the last of the three originally-pending corrections from earlier phases — while two watcher dashboards, Data Entry and Manager, sat open and were never reloaded) end-to-end through the now-consolidated connection: both dashboards updated automatically within the existing ~22s window (Correction Requested counts dropped to 0 on both, Open counts incremented on both — this Job Card's raw status was already past "Under Review" so it correctly moved to the Open bucket rather than Needs Your Action, matching pre-existing, unchanged business logic, not a regression) — confirming zero functional regression from the consolidation. Toast-center's own visual popup wasn't separately screenshotted, but it shares the identical, now-proven subscribe wiring as the badge and RealtimeRefresh, both of which were directly confirmed working. `AutoRefresh` (pure polling, no SSE) was untouched and remains the fallback exactly as before. No DB schema changes, no migrations, no deployment, no production access, no workflow logic touched, no data deleted, Store/Engineer not reintroduced, Offline Inventory Control untouched, Job Cards not renamed to Work Orders, "Materials" wording preserved. All checks pass: lint ✓, typecheck ✓, build ✓ (same 61-route shape).
- 2026-07-28: Phase ManagerApprovalSuccessPopup-MaterialsAwaitingReceipt-01 — **Root cause of the forced-navigation complaint**: `approveJobCardAndMaterialsAction` (`app/actions/workflow.ts`) always redirected to the full Job Card detail page regardless of where Manager triggered "Approve and Open" from (quick-view popup on the dashboard, Job Cards list, or Materials Requests list), because the Server Action's `redirect()` target was hardcoded rather than aware of the originating page. **Fix**: the quick-view's approve form now carries two hidden fields — `return_to` (the quick-view's own existing `data.closeHref`, already page-specific) and a new `return_to_param` — and the action reads them via a new `safeReturnTo()`/`safeReturnToParam()` pair (same-origin-only + a strict `"preview"`/`"jobPreview"` allowlist, to rule out an open-redirect) to send Manager back to whichever page they approved from, with `?success=job-card-opened&<param>=<id>` instead of a hard navigation. Built `JobCardOpenedModal` (new file, `components/work-orders/job-card-opened-modal.tsx`, modeled on `CorrectionRequestSentModal`/`JobCardClosedModal`) with the exact required title/message/summary/buttons — it reuses the same `QuickViewData` (`drawerData`) every host page already fetches for `?preview=`/`?jobPreview=`, so no new query was needed; wired into all three host pages (`dashboard/page.tsx`, `maintenance/work-orders/page.tsx`, `store/parts-requests/page.tsx`) as a third branch alongside each page's existing `showCreatedModal`/quick-view ternary, and added `"job-card-opened"` to `SUPPRESSED_SUCCESS_CODES` up front to avoid a repeat of the duplicate-toast bug from an earlier phase. Task 3: the approval modal's helper text now always shows one of the two required sentences (previously only showed a note when materials existed, and even then a differently-worded one) — `return_to`/`return_to_param` hidden fields were added to the same form. **Task 4 — new display-mapping layer**: added `materialsReceiptStatus()`/`materialsReceiptStatusTone()` (Requested / Awaiting Receipt / Received), built directly on the already-existing `materialsRequestJobCardHelper()`'s `canReceive` signal so the two can never disagree — pure display mapping, `parts_requests.status` itself untouched. Split `lib/display/parts-request-labels.ts` into a new client-safe `lib/display/parts-request-labels-display.ts` (mirroring the established `simplified-status.ts`/`simplified-status-display.ts` pattern) specifically so the quick-view popup (a client component) could use the exact same mapping as every server-rendered page — the original file re-exports everything plus keeps its server-only-only exports. **Task 5 — Materials Requests list page overhaul**: KPI cards and tabs changed from the old 2-way Requested/Received to Total/Awaiting Receipt/Received, with a proper join-aware count (`parts_requests.status` not-yet-received AND linked `work_orders.status` in the Open bucket) replacing the old raw-status-only count; default sort changed to Awaiting-Receipt-first/oldest-first-within-bucket via a two-phase query (lightweight id/status/job-status/date projection sorted in memory, then a full fetch for just the paginated slice — the Received tab keeps simple newest-first since every row there shares one bucket already); added a "Requested" (age, e.g. "3 days ago") column and a Materials preview line under the request number; status badge now shows the 3-value mapping instead of the old 2-value one. The existing Action-column logic (Receive Materials / Open Job Card / just Open) already matched Task 5's spec exactly and needed no changes. **Task 6 — dashboard wording**: Manager's "Materials Requests" KPI card renamed to "Materials Awaiting Receipt" with the exact required helper text and a join-aware count query (previously just `OPEN_PR_STATUSES` with no Job-Card-openness check); Data Entry's dashboard had no equivalent card at all — added a 6th KPI card (grid widened from 5 to 6 columns) scoped to the Data Entry user's own Job Cards. **Task 7 — quick-view materials panel**: section heading now switches between "Materials Requested"/"Materials Awaiting Receipt"/"Materials Received"/generic "Materials" based on the same mapping; - 2026-07-28: Phase JobCardStatusSimplification-ApprovedActive-01 — Replaced the simplified Job Card model's "Correction Requested" primary status and "Open" bucket per a management-driven UI simplicity request, without touching the backend status model. **Task 1 finding**: backend "Approved" is a real, live, distinct status (not a legacy leftover) — `approveWorkOrder` sets `work_orders.status = "Approved"` directly, and a Job Card sits there until something moves it further (materials marked Waiting Stock, technician assigned, work started, or materials fully received via `syncJobCardMaterialStatusInTx`, which auto-advances it to "Materials Issued"). This meant the cleanest possible mapping needed no schema change and no new status: backend Approved → UI "Approved"; backend Waiting Materials/Partially Issued/Materials Issued/Assigned/In Progress (`OPEN_JOB_CARD_STATUSES` minus Approved) → UI "Active"; Created → Draft; Under Review → Submitted; Closed → Closed. **Core change** (`lib/work-orders/simplified-status-display.ts`): `SimplifiedStatus` type changed from Draft/Submitted/Correction Requested/Open/Closed to Draft/Submitted/Approved/Active/Closed; added `ACTIVE_JOB_CARD_STATUSES` (the five backend statuses past Approved); `displaySimplifiedStatus()` dropped its `hasPendingCorrection` parameter entirely — a pending correction no longer overrides the primary status, so the function is now a plain `(status: string) => SimplifiedStatus`, updated at every one of its ~15 call sites across the dashboard, Job Cards list, Materials Requests list/detail, the quick-view popup, and the Job Card detail page. Added `NEEDS_UPDATE_LABEL`/`NEEDS_UPDATE_TONE` ("Needs Update", amber) as the new shared secondary-badge constant, rendered as a small second `StatusBadge` next to the primary one wherever `hasPendingCorrection`/a per-row correction-id-set match is true, instead of replacing the primary label — applied to: the Job Cards list row status cell, the quick-view popup's header badges and Current Status panel, the Job Card detail page's identity strip and Quick Facts badges, the detail page's 5-stage stepper (a small amber chip next to the current stage instead of swapping that stage's own color/label to red), the dashboard's WoRow/TechJobRow/NuJobCardRow (Engineer/Technician/Data-Entry recent-list rows), and the Materials Requests list's inline Job Card status line. `OPEN_JOB_CARD_STATUSES` itself was deliberately left unchanged (still Approved + the five Active statuses) since it's a backend-gating concept (canReceive, correction-lookup scope) unrelated to display grouping. **Job Cards list page** (`app/(dashboard)/maintenance/work-orders/page.tsx`): tabs/KPI cards changed from All/Draft/Submitted/Correction Requested/Open/Closed to All/Draft/Submitted/Approved/Active/Closed (same 6-card grid, no layout change); `getStatusMap()` gained `Submitted`/`Active` keys and kept `Open`/`Correction` as legacy/back-compat keys (unrendered, so an old bookmarked `?status=Open` or `?status=Correction` link still resolves rows instead of erroring — the Manager/Data-Entry dashboards' "View Corrections" links deliberately still point at `?status=Correction`, since that filter stays functionally necessary even though it's no longer a visible tab); the Submitted/Correction/Open where-clause special-casing (previously needed only to subtract correction-pending ids from the Submitted/Open counts) was deleted outright now that corrections no longer carve counts out of anything — `submittedCount`/`approvedCount`/`activeCount` are now plain `countFor(statusSummaries, [...])` calls, the same simple pattern `draftCount`/`closed` already used. `correctionIdSet` itself is still computed exactly as before (still needed for the per-row secondary badge and the still-functional `?status=Correction` link) — only removed the now-dead `correctionCount` variable ESLint flagged as unused once nothing displayed it as a headline number anymore. **Dashboards** (`app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`): Data Entry's "My Work Today" row replaced Correction Requested+Open with Approved+Active (BadgeCheck/Wrench icons, both blue); Manager's "Job Cards" row replaced Correction Requested+Open Job Cards with Approved+Active the same way — both dashboards' correction counts are still computed (drive the still-present, still-actionable "needs your correction"/"Waiting on Data Entry" alert banners, which Task 6 explicitly said to keep, just not as a headline KPI card). Manager's "Assign Work" Quick Action, previously linking to the now-nonexistent-as-a-tab `?status=Open`, was repointed to the already-established `?status=ReadyToAssign` virtual bucket (Approved + Materials Issued) for accuracy. The Manager dashboard's `mgActionMeta`-driven "Needs Your Action" queue is unchanged — it already excluded correction-pending Job Cards before this phase (Task 6's explicit instruction to keep that exclusion, since a correction-pending record is waiting on Data Entry, not Manager). Engineer's and Super Admin's separate "Open"/"Open Job Cards" KPI cards were deliberately left unchanged — both count a broader "not yet Closed/Rejected/Cancelled" bucket that includes Draft and Submitted records too, a different concept from the new Active bucket, so renaming them to "Active" would have misrepresented the count; neither role is one of the three active operational roles this task targets. **Correction-flow surfaces**: `CorrectionRequestSentModal`'s "Current status" badge changed from "Correction Requested" (red) to "Needs Update" (amber) for consistency with every other surface; the Job Card detail page's own actionable correction banner (the box with the question/response form) and `workflow-actions.tsx`'s "Correction Requested — Waiting on Data Entry." (...) : (...)}` (`isEmpty` = zero material records exist at all) — meaning the search bar, category filter, balance-status filter, and Reset button vanished completely whenever the balance list was empty, for both Manager and Data Entry, exactly as both symptoms described in the task. **Fix**: hoisted the "Search + filters" section to render unconditionally, immediately after the KPI cards and before any empty-state branching; the "All Materials: N" category-count line stays gated on `!isEmpty` (showing "All Materials: 0" would be redundant right above the "No materials found." heading); the results area below is now a clean three-way branch — `isEmpty` (zero materials ever registered) shows the existing "No materials found." / "Add a new material to start tracking inventory." panel with Add New Material/Receive Material buttons (wording already matched Task 5's requirement exactly, untouched), else `filteredItems.length === 0` (materials exist but search/filters excluded all of them) shows the existing "No matching materials found." panel, else the balance table.
